Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The combinatorial variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 309 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 262 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_309(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 309. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^262(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 211.2 (Structural Decomposition)
